**[00:03:36.710] Rep. Alexandria "Queenie" Gonzales** (asr-000046)

**[00:03:39.410] Rep. Alexandria "Queenie" Gonzales** (asr-000047)  
magandang hapon po sa ating lahat.

**[00:03:42.270] Rep. Alexandria "Queenie" Gonzales** (asr-000048)  
Today's budget hearing gives the committee an opportunity to examine

**[00:03:46.670] Rep. Alexandria "Queenie" Gonzales** (asr-000049)  
how the Presidential Communications Office fulfills one of the government's

**[00:03:54.890] Rep. Alexandria "Queenie" Gonzales** (asr-000050)  
most fundamental responsibilities,

**[00:03:59.870] Rep. Alexandria "Queenie" Gonzales** (asr-000051)  
keeping the people accurately, promptly, and meaningfully informed.

**[00:04:04.710] Rep. Alexandria "Queenie" Gonzales** (asr-000052)  
Under Executive Order No. 16, Series of 2023,

**[00:04:09.470] Rep. Alexandria "Queenie" Gonzales** (asr-000053)  
the PCO serves as the communication arm of the Executive Branch,

**[00:04:16.230] Rep. Alexandria "Queenie" Gonzales** (asr-000054)  
responsible for formulating, coordinating, and implementing integrated public information programs

**[00:04:19.750] Rep. Alexandria "Queenie" Gonzales** (asr-000055)  
concerning the policies, programs, and activities of the President and the National Government.

**[00:04:22.490] Rep. Alexandria "Queenie" Gonzales** (asr-000058)  
This function is essential to effective governance.

**[00:04:26.430] Rep. Alexandria "Queenie" Gonzales** (asr-000059)  
Government programs can serve the public only when citizens understand what assistance is available,

**[00:04:34.290] Rep. Alexandria "Queenie" Gonzales** (asr-000060)  
how to access it, why policies are being adopted, and how public institutions may be held accountable.

**[00:04:41.350] Rep. Alexandria "Queenie" Gonzales** (asr-000061)  
Clear communication, therefore, connects policy with public service,

**[00:04:46.990] Rep. Alexandria "Queenie" Gonzales** (asr-000062)  
supports participation, and helps sustain trust,

**[00:04:51.150] Rep. Alexandria "Queenie" Gonzales** (asr-000063)  
between the government and the people.

**[00:04:54.190] Rep. Alexandria "Queenie" Gonzales** (asr-000064)  
This responsibility has become even more significant at a time

**[00:04:58.130] Rep. Alexandria "Queenie" Gonzales** (asr-000065)  
when social media is woven into everyday life.

**[00:05:01.830] Rep. Alexandria "Queenie" Gonzales** (asr-000066)  
Information now reaches millions within seconds,

**[00:05:05.490] Rep. Alexandria "Queenie" Gonzales** (asr-000067)  
but so do misinformation, disinformation, manipulated content, and AI-generated material.

**[00:05:14.260] Rep. Alexandria "Queenie" Gonzales** (asr-000068)  
The PCO must be present where people receive and exchange information,

**[00:05:19.890] Rep. Alexandria "Queenie" Gonzales** (asr-000069)  
not merely to broadcast official messages,

**[00:05:23.170] Rep. Alexandria "Queenie" Gonzales** (asr-000070)  
but to communicate quickly, clearly, credibly, and directly affect the daily lives of the Filipinos.

**[00:06:30.010] Rep. Alexandria "Queenie" Gonzales** (asr-000071)  
The total budget of the PCO and its attached agencies and corporations

**[00:06:35.770] Rep. Alexandria "Queenie" Gonzales** (asr-000072)  
was 2.768 billion under the fiscal year 2026 NEP

**[00:06:42.870] Rep. Alexandria "Queenie" Gonzales** (asr-000073)  
and is proposed at 2.621 billion under the fiscal year 2027 NEP.

**[00:06:50.450] Rep. Alexandria "Queenie" Gonzales** (asr-000076)  
We look forward to a productive discussion on the PCO's fiscal year 2026 performance

**[00:06:57.370] Rep. Alexandria "Queenie" Gonzales** (asr-000077)  
and on the concrete outputs, safeguards, and measurable results to be delivered under its fiscal year 2027 proposal.

**[00:07:05.830] Rep. Alexandria "Queenie" Gonzales** (asr-000078)  
Our objective is to support a communication system that informs rather than confuses, engages rather than excludes,

**[00:07:13.750] Rep. Alexandria "Queenie" Gonzales** (asr-000079)  
and strengthens the people's trust in government through truth, clarity, and accountability.

**[00:07:19.250] Rep. Alexandria "Queenie" Gonzales** (asr-000080)  
Thank you and good afternoon.

**[00:07:22.860] Rep. Alexandria "Queenie" Gonzales** (asr-000081)  
Secretary Dave Gomez, sir, you may now proceed with your presentation.

**[00:07:32.030] Secretary David M. Gomez** (asr-000082)  
Thank you very much and good afternoon, Madam Chair, Honorable Members of the House Committee on Appropriations.

**[00:07:38.810] Secretary David M. Gomez** (asr-000083)  
On behalf of the Presidential Communications Office, let me express our gratitude for the gracious invitation today

**[00:07:45.410] Secretary David M. Gomez** (asr-000084)  
and for the very warm welcome to the August halls of the House of Representatives.

**[00:07:50.510] Secretary David M. Gomez** (asr-000085)  
It will be my honor, together with the heads and officials of the PCO and our attached agencies,

**[00:07:56.530] Secretary David M. Gomez** (asr-000086)  
to present and explain the rationale behind our proposed budget for fiscal year 2027.

**[00:08:03.670] Secretary David M. Gomez** (asr-000087)  
But before I proceed, Madam Chair, allow me to simply frame the context of our proposed 2027 appropriations.

**[00:08:12.630] Secretary David M. Gomez** (asr-000088)  
The PCO is proposing a budget of 2.62 billion pesos, which is 6.6% lower than our current 2026 budget.

**[00:08:26.250] Secretary David M. Gomez** (asr-000089)  
This represents a measly 0.03% of the total 7.2 trillion proposed national budget.

**[00:08:35.770] Secretary David M. Gomez** (asr-000090)  
We have the lowest budget and the last department-level agency of the Executive Department in terms of budgetary allocation.

**[00:08:44.410] Secretary David M. Gomez** (asr-000091)  
We may have the least in the budget priority, but we assure your honors that the PCO and our attached agencies are up to the Herculean task of engaging and involving the citizenry,

**[00:08:56.010] Secretary David M. Gomez** (asr-000092)  
and the mass media in enriching the quality of public discourse on all matters of governance.

**[00:09:03.690] Secretary David M. Gomez** (asr-000093)  
Next slide, please.

**[00:09:05.570] Secretary David M. Gomez** (asr-000094)  
As I have just mentioned, the PCO family's budget proposal is 2.62 billion pesos, or around 0.03% of the 7.2 trillion national expenditure program for fiscal year 2027.

**[00:09:23.400] Secretary David M. Gomez** (asr-000095)  
And it is 6.6% lower.

**[00:09:25.970] Secretary David M. Gomez** (asr-000096)  
than the 2026 current level of our budget.

**[00:09:29.450] Secretary David M. Gomez** (asr-000097)  
Just very quickly, as per Executive Order No. 16, you will see that the PCO has six attached agencies and three GOCCs that are also attached to the PCO.

**[00:09:45.510] Secretary David M. Gomez** (asr-000098)  
They are the Bureau of Broadcast Services, the Bureau of Communication Services, the News and Information Bureau, the Philippine Information Agency, Radio TV Malacanang or RTVM,

**[00:09:55.890] Secretary David M. Gomez** (asr-000099)  
IBC 13, PTV 4, APO Press, and the Philippine Information Agency.

**[00:09:59.090] Secretary David M. Gomez** (asr-000100)  
We have the EPO, the Program of Production, and the National Printing Office.

**[00:10:04.160] Secretary David M. Gomez** (asr-000101)  
Here, the following are the major programs, activities, and projects to be funded by our proposed fiscal year 2027 budget.

**[00:10:14.350] Secretary David M. Gomez** (asr-000102)  
As Madam Chair pointed out, the highest in terms of our priorities, the Presidential Communications Program,

**[00:10:21.230] Secretary David M. Gomez** (asr-000103)  
is at the core of our mandate to serve as the primary communications arm of the Executive Department,

**[00:10:26.150] Secretary David M. Gomez** (asr-000104)  
ensuring that government messages are clear, consistent, and accessible to the Filipino people.

**[00:10:33.130] Secretary David M. Gomez** (asr-000117)  
It focuses on the formulation, coordination, and implementation of integrated public information plans and programs that effectively communicate the priorities, policies, and achievements of the President and the Philippine Government.

**[00:10:47.210] Secretary David M. Gomez** (asr-000118)  
It not only involves the crafting of messages, but also the careful alignment of communication strategies across all agencies to ensure coherence, clarity in how government policies and programs are conveyed to the public.

**[00:11:01.510] Secretary David M. Gomez** (asr-000119)  
In a time when information is fast-moving and often contested, the program ensures that our citizens receive timely, accurate, and relevant information straight from the government.

**[00:11:12.770] Secretary David M. Gomez** (asr-000120)  
It is anchored on our sector outcome, which is people-centered, innovative, clean, efficient, effective, and inclusive delivery of public goods and services.

**[00:11:24.310] Secretary David M. Gomez** (asr-000121)  
Strategic communications plays a vital role in realizing this outcome.

**[00:11:28.990] Secretary David M. Gomez** (asr-000122)  
By delivering clear,

**[00:11:31.130] Secretary David M. Gomez** (asr-000123)  
reliable information, we strengthen transparency and accountability in governance, while ensuring that services and programs are better understood and appreciated by the people they are intended to serve.

**[00:11:43.810] Secretary David M. Gomez** (asr-000124)  
Communication, therefore, becomes a tool not only for information sharing, but also for enabling citizens to fully benefit from government programs and participate in the democratic process.

**[00:11:55.750] Secretary David M. Gomez** (asr-000125)  
In line with this, the organizational outcome that we seek to achieve in public access,

**[00:12:00.510] Secretary David M. Gomez** (asr-000126)  
engagement, and participation in the democratic process,

**[00:12:01.430] Secretary David M. Gomez** (asr-000127)  
and understanding of presidential policies and government programs.

**[00:12:04.830] Secretary David M. Gomez** (asr-000128)  
This means that we are not only ensuring that information reaches the people, but also that it resonates with them and encourages meaningful dialogue.

**[00:12:14.970] Secretary David M. Gomez** (asr-000129)  
Through accessible and inclusive communication, we empower citizens to engage in public discourse, to raise their concerns, and to take part in nation-building.

**[00:12:23.830] Secretary David M. Gomez** (asr-000130)  
In doing so, the program fosters a deeper appreciation of government initiatives and helps bridge the gap between policy formulation and citizen experience.

**[00:12:35.370] Secretary David M. Gomez** (asr-000137)  
To realize these outcomes, we are implementing several concrete activities.

**[00:12:39.890] Secretary David M. Gomez** (asr-000138)  
Among this is the freedom of information program, which upholds transparency by granting citizens greater access to government data and information.

**[00:12:48.010] Secretary David M. Gomez** (asr-000139)  
We also conduct regular press briefings to provide timely updates and clarifications on the president's directives and government initiatives.

**[00:12:56.530] Secretary David M. Gomez** (asr-000140)  
To strengthen information literacy, we have launched the Maging Mapanuri

**[00:13:00.770] Secretary David M. Gomez** (asr-000141)  
program, a series of fact-checking

**[00:13:02.950] Secretary David M. Gomez** (asr-000142)  
workshops designed to combat disinformation

**[00:13:04.790] Secretary David M. Gomez** (asr-000143)  
and promote responsible

**[00:13:06.670] Secretary David M. Gomez** (asr-000144)  
news consumption. We

**[00:13:08.390] Secretary David M. Gomez** (asr-000145)  
likewise provide various communication

**[00:13:10.410] Secretary David M. Gomez** (asr-000146)  
workshops and capability-building activities

**[00:13:12.970] Secretary David M. Gomez** (asr-000147)  
for state media

**[00:13:14.290] Secretary David M. Gomez** (asr-000148)  
and the covering media, enhancing

**[00:13:16.570] Secretary David M. Gomez** (asr-000149)  
their skills in messaging, crisis

**[00:13:18.470] Secretary David M. Gomez** (asr-000150)  
communications, and innovative

**[00:13:20.070] Secretary David M. Gomez** (asr-000151)  
content delivery. The program

**[00:13:22.630] Secretary David M. Gomez** (asr-000152)  
also undertakes the coverage

**[00:13:24.630] Secretary David M. Gomez** (asr-000153)  
of presidential engagements, both

**[00:13:26.550] Secretary David M. Gomez** (asr-000154)  
local and international, ensuring

**[00:13:28.790] Secretary David M. Gomez** (asr-000155)  
that the Filipino people are kept

**[00:13:30.670] Secretary David M. Gomez** (asr-000156)  
informed of the President's

**[00:13:32.490] Secretary David M. Gomez** (asr-000157)  
activities in the country and his

**[00:13:34.290] Secretary David M. Gomez** (asr-000158)  
representation of national interest

**[00:13:36.670] Secretary David M. Gomez** (asr-000159)  
on the global stage.

**[00:13:39.070] Secretary David M. Gomez** (asr-000160)  
In addition,

**[00:13:40.770] Secretary David M. Gomez** (asr-000161)  
the program supports collaborative projects

**[00:13:42.610] Secretary David M. Gomez** (asr-000162)  
with other government agencies

**[00:13:44.670] Secretary David M. Gomez** (asr-000163)  
such as campaigns with the Department of Energy

**[00:13:46.910] Secretary David M. Gomez** (asr-000164)  
on Energy Conservation

**[00:13:48.550] Secretary David M. Gomez** (asr-000165)  
and Efficiency, which help bring

**[00:13:50.610] Secretary David M. Gomez** (asr-000166)  
national priorities closer to the people.

**[00:13:52.750] Secretary David M. Gomez** (asr-000167)  
This interagency effort

**[00:13:54.630] Secretary David M. Gomez** (asr-000168)  
demonstrates our commitment

**[00:13:56.510] Secretary David M. Gomez** (asr-000169)  
to a whole-of-government approach

**[00:13:58.550] Secretary David M. Gomez** (asr-000170)  
to communication, ensuring

**[00:14:00.490] Secretary David M. Gomez** (asr-000171)  
that important programs in

**[00:14:02.430] Secretary David M. Gomez** (asr-000172)  
energy, health, agriculture,

**[00:14:04.790] Secretary David M. Gomez** (asr-000173)  
social protection, and other key sectors

**[00:14:06.810] Secretary David M. Gomez** (asr-000174)  
are clearly communicated

**[00:14:08.510] Secretary David M. Gomez** (asr-000175)  
and well understood by the public.

**[00:14:11.570] Secretary David M. Gomez** (asr-000176)  
Ultimately, the

**[00:14:12.630] Secretary David M. Gomez** (asr-000177)  
Presidential Communications Program

**[00:14:14.110] Secretary David M. Gomez** (asr-000178)  
positions communication as a bridge

**[00:14:16.770] Secretary David M. Gomez** (asr-000179)  
between the government and the people,

**[00:14:18.870] Secretary David M. Gomez** (asr-000180)  
a bridge built

**[00:14:20.750] Secretary David M. Gomez** (asr-000181)  
on trust, transparency,

**[00:14:22.370] Secretary David M. Gomez** (asr-000182)  
and accountability. It ensures

**[00:14:24.530] Secretary David M. Gomez** (asr-000183)  
that the President's policies and administration's

**[00:14:26.990] Secretary David M. Gomez** (asr-000184)  
development agenda

**[00:14:28.170] Secretary David M. Gomez** (asr-000185)  
are not only heard, but also

**[00:14:30.550] Secretary David M. Gomez** (asr-000186)  
understood, embraced, and supported

**[00:14:32.850] Secretary David M. Gomez** (asr-000187)  
by the citizenry.

**[00:14:34.930] Secretary David M. Gomez** (asr-000188)  
Next slide, please.

**[00:14:37.110] Secretary David M. Gomez** (asr-000189)  
Next, we present the programs

**[00:14:38.810] Secretary David M. Gomez** (asr-000190)  
of the attached agencies of the PCO.

**[00:14:41.630] Secretary David M. Gomez** (asr-000191)  
First, under the PBS

**[00:14:43.190] Secretary David M. Gomez** (asr-000192)  
Bureau of Broadcast Services,

**[00:14:45.230] Secretary David M. Gomez** (asr-000193)  
we implement the Public Radio

**[00:14:47.070] Secretary David M. Gomez** (asr-000194)  
Broadcasting Program, which ensures

**[00:14:49.150] Secretary David M. Gomez** (asr-000195)  
nationwide access

**[00:14:50.810] Secretary David M. Gomez** (asr-000196)  
to timely and relevant information

**[00:14:52.910] Secretary David M. Gomez** (asr-000197)

**[00:14:55.150] Secretary David M. Gomez** (asr-000198)  
Second, the Bureau of

**[00:14:56.690] Secretary David M. Gomez** (asr-000199)  
Communication Services, or BCS,

**[00:15:00.000] Secretary David M. Gomez** (asr-000201)  
We carry out the government communications program which produces print, audiovisual, and digital materials that amplify government policies, projects, and services for public awareness and understanding.

**[00:15:13.260] Secretary David M. Gomez** (asr-000202)  
The News and Information Bureau is responsible for providing efficient, effective, productive, and economical services relating to the development and formulation of domestic and foreign information program of the government.

**[00:15:27.560] Secretary David M. Gomez** (asr-000203)  
Next slide, please.

**[00:15:30.200] Secretary David M. Gomez** (asr-000204)

**[00:15:45.540] Secretary David M. Gomez** (asr-000205)

**[00:15:58.320] Secretary David M. Gomez** (asr-000206)  
while

**[00:15:59.180] Secretary David M. Gomez** (asr-000207)

**[00:16:19.220] Secretary David M. Gomez** (asr-000217)  
activities and public engagements

**[00:16:21.520] Secretary David M. Gomez** (asr-000218)

**[00:16:23.040] Secretary David M. Gomez** (asr-000219)  
Together, these programs from our

**[00:16:25.480] Secretary David M. Gomez** (asr-000220)

Just simply a slide on the

**[00:42:11.830] Secretary David M. Gomez** (asr-000957)  
138 million pesos,

**[00:42:13.870] Secretary David M. Gomez** (asr-000958)  
which shall be utilized for the

**[00:42:15.930] Secretary David M. Gomez** (asr-000959)  
revitalization program mandated

**[00:42:17.830] Secretary David M. Gomez** (asr-000960)  
under R8-10-3-9-0,

**[00:42:20.330] Secretary David M. Gomez** (asr-000961)  
encompassing the modernization

**[00:42:22.370] Secretary David M. Gomez** (asr-000962)  
of PTB4's equipment and systems

**[00:42:24.210] Secretary David M. Gomez** (asr-000963)  
as well as the digitalization

**[00:42:26.290] Secretary David M. Gomez** (asr-000964)  
of its broadcast operations.

**[00:42:28.090] Secretary David M. Gomez** (asr-000965)  
On the other hand, however,

**[00:42:29.970] Secretary David M. Gomez** (asr-000966)  
IBC-13 did not get

**[00:42:32.310] Secretary David M. Gomez** (asr-000967)  
any financial assistance

**[00:42:34.110] Secretary David M. Gomez** (asr-000968)  
or subsidy under the

**[00:42:36.150] Secretary David M. Gomez** (asr-000969)  
2027 NEP

**[00:42:38.330] Secretary David M. Gomez** (asr-000970)  
due to its continuing status

**[00:42:40.170] Secretary David M. Gomez** (asr-000971)  
as a GOCC, considered for

**[00:42:42.330] Secretary David M. Gomez** (asr-000972)  
privatization per GCG

**[00:42:44.070] Secretary David M. Gomez** (asr-000973)  
classification as of July

**[00:42:45.830] Secretary David M. Gomez** (asr-000974)  
2026. Lastly,

**[00:42:48.530] Secretary David M. Gomez** (asr-000975)  
the APO Production Unit

**[00:42:49.770] Secretary David M. Gomez** (asr-000976)  
operates solely using its corporate

**[00:42:52.170] Secretary David M. Gomez** (asr-000977)  
earnings and does not receive

**[00:42:53.850] Secretary David M. Gomez** (asr-000978)  
any subsidy from government.

**[00:42:57.170] Secretary David M. Gomez** (asr-000979)  
Next slide, please.

**[00:43:01.740] Secretary David M. Gomez** (asr-000980)  
The fiscal year 2027

**[00:43:03.180] Secretary David M. Gomez** (asr-000981)  
budget proposal of the PCO

**[00:43:04.980] Secretary David M. Gomez** (asr-000982)  
and its attached agencies in the amount

**[00:43:07.100] Secretary David M. Gomez** (asr-000983)  
of 2.4 billion reflects

**[00:43:08.960] Secretary David M. Gomez** (asr-000984)  
a decrease of around 67 million

**[00:43:11.240] Secretary David M. Gomez** (asr-000985)  
or 2.6 percent lower

**[00:43:13.220] Secretary David M. Gomez** (asr-000986)  
compared to fiscal year

**[00:43:14.940] Secretary David M. Gomez** (asr-000987)  
2026 budget

**[00:43:17.100] Secretary David M. Gomez** (asr-000988)  
level of almost

**[00:43:18.740] Secretary David M. Gomez** (asr-000989)  
2.5 billion. All received

**[00:43:21.140] Secretary David M. Gomez** (asr-000990)

**[00:43:22.780] Secretary David M. Gomez** (asr-000991)

**[00:43:24.660] Secretary David M. Gomez** (asr-000992)

**[00:43:27.980] Secretary David M. Gomez** (asr-000993)  
Next slide, please.

The proposed budgetary support to our government-owned

**[00:43:32.180] Secretary David M. Gomez** (asr-000995)  
television networks for fiscal year

**[00:43:33.940] Secretary David M. Gomez** (asr-000996)  
2027 amounts to 138

**[00:43:36.560] Secretary David M. Gomez** (asr-000997)  
million, which is about

**[00:43:37.480] Secretary David M. Gomez** (asr-000998)  
46.5 percent lower

**[00:43:39.880] Secretary David M. Gomez** (asr-000999)  
than the 120 million

**[00:43:42.340] Secretary David M. Gomez** (asr-001000)  
subsidy given to

**[00:43:44.140] Secretary David M. Gomez** (asr-001001)

**[00:43:48.480] Secretary David M. Gomez** (asr-001002)  
Next slide, please.

**[00:43:50.140] Secretary David M. Gomez** (asr-001003)  
Zooming in, the total proposed

**[00:43:51.620] Secretary David M. Gomez** (asr-001004)  
budget of the PCO proper for

**[00:43:53.720] Secretary David M. Gomez** (asr-001005)  
2027 under the NEP

**[00:43:56.400] Secretary David M. Gomez** (asr-001006)  
amounts to

**[00:43:57.420] Secretary David M. Gomez** (asr-001007)  
784 million,

**[00:43:59.160] Secretary David M. Gomez** (asr-001008)

**[00:44:01.740] Secretary David M. Gomez** (asr-001009)  
decrease from

**[00:44:02.800] Secretary David M. Gomez** (asr-001010)

**[00:44:06.120] Secretary David M. Gomez** (asr-001011)  
Next slide, please.

**[00:44:08.560] Secretary David M. Gomez** (asr-001012)  
The total

**[00:44:09.660] Secretary David M. Gomez** (asr-001013)  
budget of 784

**[00:44:12.180] Secretary David M. Gomez** (asr-001014)  
million pesos as proposed

**[00:44:13.840] Secretary David M. Gomez** (asr-001015)  
under 2027 NEP

**[00:44:15.600] Secretary David M. Gomez** (asr-001016)  
constituted around 19 percent

**[00:44:17.920] Secretary David M. Gomez** (asr-001017)  
decrease from fiscal year 2026

**[00:44:20.020] Secretary David M. Gomez** (asr-001018)  
NEP and

**[00:44:21.380] Secretary David M. Gomez** (asr-001019)  
2027 NEP

**[00:44:23.180] Secretary David M. Gomez** (asr-001020)  
and a GAA level of

**[00:44:26.660] Secretary David M. Gomez** (asr-001021)  
972 million,

**[00:44:27.180] Secretary David M. Gomez** (asr-001022)  
or about 37.6

**[00:44:29.400] Secretary David M. Gomez** (asr-001023)  
lower than our original

**[00:44:31.020] Secretary David M. Gomez** (asr-001024)  
2027 proposal

**[00:44:33.040] Secretary David M. Gomez** (asr-001025)

**[00:44:35.160] Secretary David M. Gomez** (asr-001026)  
this year. The decrease

**[00:44:37.120] Secretary David M. Gomez** (asr-001027)  
of around 187

**[00:44:39.360] Secretary David M. Gomez** (asr-001028)  
million was due to the net effect

**[00:44:41.000] Secretary David M. Gomez** (asr-001029)

**[00:44:43.300] Secretary David M. Gomez** (asr-001030)  
services due to the

**[00:44:45.280] Secretary David M. Gomez** (asr-001031)

**[00:44:47.260] Secretary David M. Gomez** (asr-001032)  
DBM-approved contractual positions

**[00:44:48.920] Secretary David M. Gomez** (asr-001033)  
under the Digital Management Services

**[00:44:51.140] Secretary David M. Gomez** (asr-001034)  
Communications and Content Production

**[00:44:53.140] Secretary David M. Gomez** (asr-001035)  
clusters to augment the

**[00:44:55.320] Secretary David M. Gomez** (asr-001036)  
existing PCO staff and performing agencies'

**[00:44:57.200] Secretary David M. Gomez** (asr-001037)  
functions.

**[00:44:57.940] Secretary David M. Gomez** (asr-001038)  
and the decrease in the maintenance and other operating expenses and capital outlay of P235 million and P223 million respectively

**[00:45:07.600] Secretary David M. Gomez** (asr-001039)  
since the DBM did not consider the inclusion in the fiscal year 2027 NEP of Tier 2 projects that we propose

**[00:45:17.240] Secretary David M. Gomez** (asr-001040)  
which are mostly ongoing PAP funded under fiscal year 2026.

**[00:45:23.090] Secretary David M. Gomez** (asr-001041)  
Next slide please.

**[00:45:25.070] Secretary David M. Gomez** (asr-001042)  
As to the various budget dimensions of the said amount, the succeeding slides will show the breakdown of the proposed budget allocations.

**[00:45:33.950] Secretary David M. Gomez** (asr-001043)  
Next slide please.

**[00:45:35.880] Secretary David M. Gomez** (asr-001044)  
By allotment class, 63% of this amount will go to MOOE while personal services, PS will have a share of 37%.

**[00:45:46.780] Secretary David M. Gomez** (asr-001045)  
We were not allocated any capital allotment for this year as mentioned earlier.

**[00:45:55.180] Secretary David M. Gomez** (asr-001046)  
Next slide.

**[00:45:56.160] Secretary David M. Gomez** (asr-001047)  
For personal services, the proposed budget for personal services amounting to P289 million pertains to PS requirements for 185 unfilled positions,

**[00:46:08.720] Secretary David M. Gomez** (asr-001048)  
provisions for salaries of 17 non-permanent positions, terminal leave benefits, provision for loyalty award and anniversary bonus of the milestone year of the PCO in 2027.

**[00:46:22.200] Secretary David M. Gomez** (asr-001049)  
For MOOE, the proposed budget of P400 million.

**[00:46:27.720] Secretary David M. Gomez** (asr-001050)  
95 million pertains to operating requirements for the formulation, coordination, and implementation of the integrated public information plans and programs,

**[00:46:35.940] Secretary David M. Gomez** (asr-001051)  
our core operations under the Presidential Communications Program,

**[00:46:40.100] Secretary David M. Gomez** (asr-001052)  
and the operating requirements for our general administration and support and general services.

**[00:46:47.480] Secretary David M. Gomez** (asr-001053)  
Next slide please.

**[00:46:49.780] Secretary David M. Gomez** (asr-001054)  
Appropriation by source.

**[00:46:51.240] Secretary David M. Gomez** (asr-001055)  
98% of the total amount reflected in the PCO proposed budget pertains to agency-specific appropriations,

**[00:46:59.000] Secretary David M. Gomez** (asr-001056)  
directly intended for our operational expenses, and the implementation of core programs and various programs.

**[00:47:04.660] Secretary David M. Gomez** (asr-001057)  
A very minimal portion of the total proposed amount, or 2%, will be part of the automatic appropriations corresponding to the government's share of the retirement and life insurance premiums being remitted to the GSIS.

**[00:47:21.380] Secretary David M. Gomez** (asr-001058)  
Next slide.

**[00:47:23.230] Secretary David M. Gomez** (asr-001059)  
In closing, Your Honorable Chairperson, Distinguished Members of the Committee,

**[00:47:28.570] Secretary David M. Gomez** (asr-001060)  
thank you for your kind attention and for allowing us to present.

**[00:47:32.010] Secretary David M. Gomez** (asr-001061)  
The proposed budget of the PCO for fiscal year 2027.

**[00:49:15.860] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-001100)  
Secretary Dave Gomez,

**[00:49:18.040] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-001101)  
Usec Mon Ilagan.

**[00:49:19.780] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-001102)  
Hindi ko na po babatiin lahat,

**[00:49:21.420] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-001103)

**[00:49:23.320] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-001104)

**[00:49:26.400] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-001105)

**[00:49:29.800] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-001106)  
Why is the fiscal year 2027

**[00:49:32.420] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-001107)  
proposed budget for the PCO

**[00:49:34.520] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-001108)  
and attached agencies only

**[00:49:36.500] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-001109)  
2.483 billion?

**[00:49:39.120] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-001110)  
And why were some offices reduced

**[00:49:41.220] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-001111)  
while the Philippine Information Agency

**[00:49:44.040] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-001112)

**[00:49:45.500] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-001113)  
What specific programs justify

**[00:49:47.740] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-001114)  
the increase of the PIA?

**[00:49:49.410] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-001115)  
What's...

**[00:49:52.310] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-001116)  
Ano po ba yung ano?

**[00:49:53.790] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-001117)  
Specific programs na dahilan

**[00:49:56.990] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-001118)  
kung bakit in-increase po natin yung PIA

**[00:49:59.570] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-001119)  
tapos yung iba binawasan?

**[00:50:02.250] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-001120)  
Madam Chair, with your permission,

**[00:50:04.070] Secretary David M. Gomez** (asr-001121)  
maraming salamat po kung

**[00:50:05.570] Secretary David M. Gomez** (asr-001122)  
kung it's really a very,

**[00:50:07.610] Secretary David M. Gomez** (asr-001123)  
very good question that provides me

**[00:50:10.130] Secretary David M. Gomez** (asr-001124)  
the opportunity to...

**[00:50:10.550] Secretary David M. Gomez** (asr-001125)  
to clarify your points.

**[00:50:13.950] Secretary David M. Gomez** (asr-001126)  
Well, for the PCO proper,

**[00:50:15.630] Secretary David M. Gomez** (asr-001127)  
yes, we experienced a reduction

**[00:50:17.530] Secretary David M. Gomez** (asr-001128)  
in our fiscal year 2027 budget

**[00:50:20.070] Secretary David M. Gomez** (asr-001129)  
simply because our budget this year

**[00:50:22.910] Secretary David M. Gomez** (asr-001130)  
was a bit higher

**[00:50:24.170] Secretary David M. Gomez** (asr-001131)  
because of the hosting of the ASEAN.

**[00:50:26.130] Secretary David M. Gomez** (asr-001132)  
So naturally, by 2027,

**[00:50:28.970] Secretary David M. Gomez** (asr-001133)  
we really expected the budget

**[00:50:30.430] Secretary David M. Gomez** (asr-001134)  
to be reduced.

**[00:50:32.010] Secretary David M. Gomez** (asr-001135)  
Now, as for the PIA po,

**[00:50:33.250] Secretary David M. Gomez** (asr-001136)  
and my colleagues from the PIA are here

**[00:50:36.130] Secretary David M. Gomez** (asr-001137)  
and they are ready to further expound

**[00:50:38.850] Secretary David M. Gomez** (asr-001138)  
on my response.

**[00:50:41.350] Secretary David M. Gomez** (asr-001139)  
Yung building po kasi natin ng PIA dito,

**[00:50:44.130] Secretary David M. Gomez** (asr-001140)  
yung kapitbahin na sa may Visayas Avenue,

**[00:50:47.590] Secretary David M. Gomez** (asr-001141)  
is already 44-year-old po

**[00:50:50.190] Secretary David M. Gomez** (asr-001142)  
and it's talagang...

**[00:50:51.210] Secretary David M. Gomez** (asr-001143)  
it's in a very bad and sad state

**[00:50:54.170] Secretary David M. Gomez** (asr-001144)  
that the building really requires retrofitting.

**[00:50:57.970] Secretary David M. Gomez** (asr-001145)  
So most significant part of the increase

**[00:51:03.190] Secretary David M. Gomez** (asr-001146)  
in the PIA budget

**[00:51:04.150] Secretary David M. Gomez** (asr-001147)  
really will go to capital outlay

**[00:51:06.390] Secretary David M. Gomez** (asr-001148)  
for the proposed retrofitting

**[00:51:08.230] Secretary David M. Gomez** (asr-001149)  
of the PIA building po.

**[00:51:11.490] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-001150)  
Bakit naman po na-slash yung

**[00:51:13.530] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-001151)  
Bureau of Broadcast Services,

**[00:51:17.050] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-001152)  
Bureau of Communication Services,

**[00:51:20.390] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-001153)  
NIB,

**[00:51:21.590] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-001154)  
RTVM,

**[00:51:24.470] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-001155)  
and then I noticed also that

**[00:51:27.730] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-001156)  
IBC 13, wala pong nakalagay.

**[00:51:30.770] Secretary David M. Gomez** (asr-001157)  
Oo nga po,

**[00:51:31.810] Secretary David M. Gomez** (asr-001158)  
medyo nakakalungkot nga po na

**[00:51:33.810] Secretary David M. Gomez** (asr-001159)  
paglabas po ng aming NEP sa DBMA,

**[00:51:37.070] Secretary David M. Gomez** (asr-001160)  
na zero po yung budget ng IBC 13.

**[00:51:40.590] Secretary David M. Gomez** (asr-001161)  
Kung maasa po kami

**[00:51:41.930] Secretary David M. Gomez** (asr-001162)  
at we pray that this committee

**[00:51:44.310] Secretary David M. Gomez** (asr-001163)  
would restore at least

**[00:51:46.130] Secretary David M. Gomez** (asr-001164)  
yung hinihingi po namin

**[00:51:47.590] Secretary David M. Gomez** (asr-001165)  
from our original NEP.

**[00:51:49.510] Secretary David M. Gomez** (asr-001166)  
Ang dahilan pong binigay sa amin

**[00:51:51.270] Secretary David M. Gomez** (asr-001167)  
kasi daw naka-plano na

**[00:51:53.950] Secretary David M. Gomez** (asr-001168)  
at nakaprograma na yung privatization

**[00:51:55.570] Secretary David M. Gomez** (asr-001169)  
ng IBC 13,

**[00:51:56.750] Secretary David M. Gomez** (asr-001170)  
which we are actually in the process

**[00:51:59.430] Secretary David M. Gomez** (asr-001171)  
of fulfilling that mandate.

**[00:52:03.170] Secretary David M. Gomez** (asr-001172)  
In fact, next week ho,

**[00:52:04.630] Secretary David M. Gomez** (asr-001173)  
I'll be meeting with the GCG

**[00:52:06.910] Secretary David M. Gomez** (asr-001174)  
to finalize the privatization plan

**[00:52:09.090] Secretary David M. Gomez** (asr-001175)  
for IBC 13.

**[00:52:11.110] Secretary David M. Gomez** (asr-001176)  
Unfortunately,

**[00:52:12.310] Secretary David M. Gomez** (asr-001177)  
marami pa pong work streams

**[00:52:14.990] Secretary David M. Gomez** (asr-001178)  
yung required before we can privatize.

**[00:52:18.590] Secretary David M. Gomez** (asr-001179)  
We're waiting for the privatization plan

**[00:52:21.530] Secretary David M. Gomez** (asr-001180)  
of the station itself.

**[00:52:23.910] Secretary David M. Gomez** (asr-001181)  
We're asking the DBP

**[00:52:26.250] Secretary David M. Gomez** (asr-001182)  
to set a valuation

**[00:52:29.450] Secretary David M. Gomez** (asr-001183)  
for the bidding price

**[01:18:30.360] Rep. Alexandria "Queenie" Gonzales** (asr-002032)  
Thank you, Representative Victoria, Vice Chair. Sorry, Representative Victoria.

**[01:18:39.460] Rep. Alexandria "Queenie" Gonzales** (asr-002033)  
So next, we now recognize Representative Brian Llamanzariz.

**[01:18:47.490] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-002034)  
Maraming salamat po, Madam Chair.

**[01:18:50.150] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-002035)  
First, I'd like to thank our Secretary and

**[01:18:54.930] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-002036)  
the whole family here because of their work, a lot of the things that we do here in Congress gets noticed by the nation.

**[01:19:04.410] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-002037)  
Kaya nagpapasalamat po ako.

**[01:19:06.010] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-002038)  
And on that note, there are certain things that I want to point out regarding the budget.

**[01:19:11.570] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-002039)  
First is regarding IBC -13's budget.

**[01:19:15.310] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-002040)  
Tama po ba yung binabasa ko dito?

**[01:19:17.610] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-002041)  
From 122 million pesos in the 2026 current program, it's now at zero pesos for 2027, correct po?

**[01:19:27.950] Secretary David M. Gomez** (asr-002043)  
That's correct, Your Honor.

**[01:19:28.970] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-002042)  
Okay. So that's a 100% decrease in IBC-13's budget, tama?

**[01:19:34.670] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-002044)  
Which is baffling to me because I feel that IBC 13 has been a critical partner in improving the trust and approval of government, especially of Congress.

**[01:19:50.510] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-002045)  
I believe that recently, the surveys have shown that Congress's numbers

**[01:19:55.310] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-002046)  
have gone up in terms of trust and approval

**[01:19:57.570] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-002047)  
because there are many programs now in government that show the hard work of congressmen.

**[01:20:02.930] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-002048)  
Tama po ba, Secretary?

**[01:20:04.010] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-002049)  
I completely agree, Your Honor.

**[01:20:06.270] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-002050)  
So, if that's the case, then why are we removing budget for a program that's working?

**[01:20:10.890] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-002051)  
If not, we should be increasing the budget.

**[01:20:13.810] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-002052)  
Madam Chair, just to give context to the committee,

**[01:20:16.210] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-002053)  
because we should be benchmarking across ASEAN to see what our neighbors are spending.

**[01:20:22.630] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-002054)  
Madam Chair, do you know how much Indonesia is spending

**[01:20:25.690] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-002055)  
for their state media? For Televisi Republic Indonesia, it's roughly 4 billion pesos.

**[01:20:31.530] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-002056)  
For Radio Republic Indonesia, it's 3 .4 billion pesos.

**[01:20:36.530] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-002057)  
Here, we're only talking about hundreds of millions of pesos for our state media,

**[01:20:40.930] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-002058)  
and we can't even give it to them, Madam Chair.

**[01:20:43.530] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-002059)  
Which is why I wanted to point this glaring decrease in the budget of our communications.

**[01:20:50.610] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-002060)  
The other thing I wanted to point out is that there was a request,

**[01:20:54.510] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-002061)  
Although I don't think it was granted,

**[01:20:56.690] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-002062)  
that the IBC budget be an additional 479 million for personnel, MOE, and even Kong TV programs.

**[01:21:08.170] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-002063)  
Is this correct, Mr. Secretary?

**[01:21:10.110] Secretary David M. Gomez** (asr-002064)  
I believe so, Your Honor, yes.

**[01:21:12.410] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-002065)  
Why do we need money for personnel and maintenance and operations?

**[01:21:16.590] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-002066)  
Can you talk about that a little bit?

**[01:21:17.890] Secretary David M. Gomez** (asr-002067)  
Thank you very much.

**[01:21:19.910] Secretary David M. Gomez** (asr-002068)  
As I was discussing earlier,

**[01:21:23.030] Secretary David M. Gomez** (asr-002069)  
we were really the first to be surprised when we saw the NEP

**[01:21:26.310] Secretary David M. Gomez** (asr-002070)  
and our proposed budget for IBC 13 reduced to zero.

**[01:21:31.970] Secretary David M. Gomez** (asr-002071)  
And if I may just repeat what I said earlier,

**[01:21:35.230] Secretary David M. Gomez** (asr-002072)  
when we inquired, the premise was because the station was up for privatization.

**[01:21:41.890] Secretary David M. Gomez** (asr-002073)  
And as I was explaining earlier, with the few months remaining for the year,

**[01:21:48.770] Secretary David M. Gomez** (asr-002074)  
it may be unlikely that we may be able to conclude

**[01:21:52.710] Secretary David M. Gomez** (asr-002075)  
the privatization by December 31st this year.

**[01:21:55.930] Secretary David M. Gomez** (asr-002076)  
And with zero budget, what does that leave us in terms of salaries of our people,

**[01:22:01.670] Secretary David M. Gomez** (asr-002077)  
the upkeep of the station?

**[01:22:03.330] Secretary David M. Gomez** (asr-002078)  
So we're really appealing to the committee if we can restore a certain amount of the budget

**[01:22:11.670] Secretary David M. Gomez** (asr-002079)  
originally requested by the station with the Department of Budget and Management.

**[01:22:16.230] Secretary David M. Gomez** (asr-002080)  
The figure that you mentioned, 460 plus, I believe is really to cover for that

**[01:22:22.510] Secretary David M. Gomez** (asr-002081)  
amount that was cut.

**[01:22:24.530] Secretary David M. Gomez** (asr-002082)  
But I'd like to also, with your permission,

**[01:22:27.550] Secretary David M. Gomez** (asr-002083)  
recognize the president of IBC 13 who can better explain what that 460 plus,

**[01:22:33.190] Secretary David M. Gomez** (asr-002084)  
which I believe he communicated already to the committee in a form of a request.

**[01:22:39.390] Secretary David M. Gomez** (asr-002085)  
Thank you, Your Honor.

**[01:22:40.890] Rep. Alexandria "Queenie" Gonzales** (asr-002086)  
Thank you.

**[01:22:45.770] IBC President Jose C. "Jimmy" Policarpio Jr.** (asr-002087)  
With your respect, Secretary Dave, Madam Chairperson, honorable members of the committee,

**[01:22:51.930] IBC President Jose C. "Jimmy" Policarpio Jr.** (asr-002088)  
we submitted the budget earlier around 660,

**[01:22:55.690] IBC President Jose C. "Jimmy" Policarpio Jr.** (asr-002089)  
but based on the representation we made, we had to reduce and set aside our CAPEX.

**[01:23:03.150] IBC President Jose C. "Jimmy" Policarpio Jr.** (asr-002090)  
We only, we had to maintain 131 million for personal services,

**[01:23:09.410] IBC President Jose C. "Jimmy" Policarpio Jr.** (asr-002091)  
MOE of 223. It's

**[01:23:13.370] IBC President Jose C. "Jimmy" Policarpio Jr.** (asr-002092)  
quite big, Your Honor, because we are maintaining 11 stations,

**[01:23:18.290] IBC President Jose C. "Jimmy" Policarpio Jr.** (asr-002093)  
we are maintaining 23 programs.

**[01:23:23.030] IBC President Jose C. "Jimmy" Policarpio Jr.** (asr-002094)  
15 and then one radio station, Your Honor.

**[01:23:25.970] IBC President Jose C. "Jimmy" Policarpio Jr.** (asr-002095)  
And likewise, we have to answer in court a 50 million arbitration award

**[01:23:34.070] IBC President Jose C. "Jimmy" Policarpio Jr.** (asr-002096)  
given to the retired employees that they submitted to arbitration.

**[01:23:44.450] IBC President Jose C. "Jimmy" Policarpio Jr.** (asr-002097)  
So instead of paying more than 100 million, they agreed to around 49 million or rounded to 50 million.

**[01:23:52.430] IBC President Jose C. "Jimmy" Policarpio Jr.** (asr-002098)  
For Congress TV, we have to allocate it also.

**[01:23:56.610] IBC President Jose C. "Jimmy" Policarpio Jr.** (asr-002099)  
Otherwise, we have been doing a lot of service to Congress, likewise to the government,

**[01:24:03.030] IBC President Jose C. "Jimmy" Policarpio Jr.** (asr-002100)  
and Congress TV is only one of the programs that we are supporting.

**[01:24:06.210] IBC President Jose C. "Jimmy" Policarpio Jr.** (asr-002101)  
Together with the Senate, together with the e -government, e -gov that we maintain,

**[01:24:13.790] IBC President Jose C. "Jimmy" Policarpio Jr.** (asr-002102)  
and other government -related programs, around 23 of them,

**[01:24:20.510] IBC President Jose C. "Jimmy" Policarpio Jr.** (asr-002103)  
all station -produced programs, Your Honor.

**[01:24:24.830] IBC President Jose C. "Jimmy" Policarpio Jr.** (asr-002104)  
Thank you.

**[01:24:26.350] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-002105)  
Madam Chair, I want to point out that IBC 13 has managed its fiscal responsibilities well.

**[01:24:36.210] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-002106)  
and has been able to take care of its employees.

**[01:24:38.290] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-002107)  
I don't understand why we're removing budget from one of the working government offices that's

**[01:24:43.750] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-002108)  
able to manage their funds properly and take care of their employees.

**[01:24:47.910] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-002109)  
We should be supporting this and financing it fully, if you ask me. They're

**[01:24:52.210] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-002110)  
handling their budget better than most.

**[01:24:54.570] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-002111)  
But, Madam Chair, there's

**[01:24:56.050] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-002112)  
so many problems here. Let's

**[01:24:57.510] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-002113)  
look at another one.

**[01:24:59.810] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-002114)  
For PIA, or the Philippine Information Agency, I believe,

**[01:25:03.530] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-002115)  
There is a request for an additional 20 million pesos for rent.

**[01:25:10.170] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-002116)  
I believe there was budget given to rehabilitate their old building,

**[01:25:15.890] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-002117)  
but the DBM forgot to give them budget to rent a new office.

**[01:25:20.690] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-002118)  
So where would all of our government employees go

**[01:25:23.730] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-002119)  
if we don't give them this budget?

**[01:25:25.970] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-002120)  
Can someone tell me a bit more about the situation here?

**[01:25:29.750] Secretary David M. Gomez** (asr-002121)  
Thank you, Your Honor.

**[01:25:30.670] Secretary David M. Gomez** (asr-002122)  
I can confirm that, Your Honor.

**[01:25:31.890] Secretary David M. Gomez** (asr-002123)  
The BM approved the request of the PIA for a budget for the retrofitting of their building,

**[01:25:38.250] Secretary David M. Gomez** (asr-002124)  
a 44 -year -old building that's really in a very sad state at the moment.

**[01:25:43.490] Secretary David M. Gomez** (asr-002125)  
We finally got the budget for retrofitting.

**[01:25:47.730] Secretary David M. Gomez** (asr-002126)  
Unfortunately, the 20 million budget that the agency is asking for rent was not included.

**[01:25:54.450] Secretary David M. Gomez** (asr-002127)  
So,

**[01:25:55.030] Secretary David M. Gomez** (asr-002128)  
of course, when construction starts

**[01:25:57.990] Secretary David M. Gomez** (asr-002129)  
and they have no place to go,

**[01:26:00.550] Secretary David M. Gomez** (asr-002130)  
I mean,

**[01:26:01.150] Secretary David M. Gomez** (asr-002131)  
they cannot stop working or just work from home.

**[01:26:04.810] Secretary David M. Gomez** (asr-002132)  
We really need that money to look for office spaces

**[01:26:08.590] Secretary David M. Gomez** (asr-002133)  
to accommodate our staff from the PIA

**[01:26:11.570] Secretary David M. Gomez** (asr-002134)  
to work in while construction is ongoing

**[01:26:14.870] Secretary David M. Gomez** (asr-002135)  
or retrofitting of the new building is ongoing.

**[01:26:18.370] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-002136)  
Madam Chair,

**[01:26:19.190] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-002137)  
I will not allow our PIA staff to go homeless.

**[01:26:25.010] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-002138)  
And I'm sure this was just an oversight on the part of the DBM

**[01:26:29.030] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-002139)  
because the intention to secure better facilities for the PIA is there. It's

**[01:26:35.130] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-002140)  
just that they didn't notice that the MOE budget was lacking 20 million pesos just for rent.

**[01:26:41.810] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-002141)  
And I think this is an easy fix for us this appropriation season.

**[01:26:45.490] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-002142)  
Although I do want to weigh retrofitting versus just having a new building altogether.

**[01:26:53.470] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-002143)  
Retrofitting a 40 plus year old building doesn't

**[01:26:57.850] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-002146)  
sound very sound.

**[01:26:58.570] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-002147)  
And I've seen the pictures and documentation.

**[01:27:01.190] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-002148)  
Tama, this is the building that has bats in it, no?

**[01:27:04.550] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-002149)  
They have a bat cave, Your Honor.

**[01:27:06.530] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-002150)  
An actual bat cave?

**[01:27:08.390] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-002151)  
Bat cave.

**[01:27:09.410] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-002152)  
Do we have any pictures of that, Mr. Secretary? I'm

**[01:27:14.030] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-002153)  
sure members of this committee don't believe how bad the condition is of the PIA.

**[01:27:18.450] Rep. Alexandria "Queenie" Gonzales** (asr-002154)  
If I may ask representatives of the PIA to be recognized, Your Honor.

**[01:27:23.850] Rep. Alexandria "Queenie" Gonzales** (asr-002155)  
Go ahead.

**[01:27:25.930] Rep. Alexandria "Queenie" Gonzales** (asr-002156)  
The representative from PIA.

**[01:27:28.030] PIA Assistant Director-General for External Affairs Kristine Venet Q. Andal** (asr-002157)  
Hi, good afternoon. Good afternoon, Kong.

**[01:27:30.710] PIA Assistant Director-General for External Affairs Kristine Venet Q. Andal** (asr-002158)  
Yes, we have photos po sa presentation as well as videos na meron talaga mga bats po doon sa building.

**[01:27:37.330] PIA Assistant Director-General for External Affairs Kristine Venet Q. Andal** (asr-002159)  
And first thing po na ginawa ko nung umupo ko sa PIA, nag -letter kami with DENR.

**[01:27:43.830] PIA Assistant Director-General for External Affairs Kristine Venet Q. Andal** (asr-002160)  
Tinutulungan kami ng DENR ngayon na mapaalis yung mga bats.

**[01:27:47.290] PIA Assistant Director-General for External Affairs Kristine Venet Q. Andal** (asr-002161)  
And kung hindi pa rin mapaalis, manual na po talaga nilang kukunin yung bats

**[01:27:52.390] PIA Assistant Director-General for External Affairs Kristine Venet Q. Andal** (asr-002162)  
pag nag -start na po yung rehab ng building,

**[01:27:54.950] PIA Assistant Director-General for External Affairs Kristine Venet Q. Andal** (asr-002163)  
Kong. Madam Chair.

**[01:27:56.710] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-002164)  
to see this is exactly why we need to fix this,

**[01:28:00.370] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-002165)  
either one of two things are gonna happen here,

**[01:28:02.150] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-002166)  
either magkaroon tayo ng Batman or ng COVID ulit

**[01:28:04.770] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-002167)  
kaya ayusin natin yan

**[01:28:07.610] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-002168)  
so the other thing that I wanted to point out is that we have a new GM for PTV4

**[01:28:13.990] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-002169)  
and a visionary at that who comes from the industry

**[01:28:18.210] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-002170)  
and the problem is, we bring in a new GM with a new task of revolutionizing PTV4

**[01:28:24.170] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-002171)  
and then we tell Tell him, by the way, there's no budget.

**[01:28:26.850] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-002172)  
How does that work?

**[01:28:28.870] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-002173)  
If I may ask, GM, you've, in your own private capacity, you've been meeting with industry leaders,

**[01:28:36.310] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-002174)  
not just in the Philippines, but around the world.

**[01:28:38.390] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-002175)  
What do they think about the current condition of PTV4?

**[01:28:42.990] PTV General Manager Lino S. Cayetano** (asr-002176)  
Thank you, Madam Chair.

**[01:28:46.210] PTV General Manager Lino S. Cayetano** (asr-002177)  
Una -una, thank you. I'm

**[01:28:47.790] PTV General Manager Lino S. Cayetano** (asr-002178)  
among some of the best communicators in Congress. Congress. I've

**[01:28:52.370] PTV General Manager Lino S. Cayetano** (asr-002179)  
worked with

**[01:28:52.970] PTV General Manager Lino S. Cayetano** (asr-002180)  
Kong Brian in the private sector

**[01:28:55.830] PTV General Manager Lino S. Cayetano** (asr-002181)  
and he's known around the communication circles as well.

**[01:28:59.110] PTV General Manager Lino S. Cayetano** (asr-002182)  
So this is exactly the space where I think he's been thriving

**[01:29:03.090] PTV General Manager Lino S. Cayetano** (asr-002183)  
so he understands.

**[01:29:04.550] PTV General Manager Lino S. Cayetano** (asr-002184)  
Just a week ago, I met with the Asia -Pacific head of BBC Studios

**[01:29:09.070] PTV General Manager Lino S. Cayetano** (asr-002185)  
and basically with all our budget, we can probably buy one show or collaborate with one show.

**[01:29:17.810] PTV General Manager Lino S. Cayetano** (asr-002186)  
So we cannot build the network with a 138 million budget.

**[01:29:22.330] PTV General Manager Lino S. Cayetano** (asr-002187)  
But for perspective also,

**[01:29:23.890] PTV General Manager Lino S. Cayetano** (asr-002188)  
you were correct to compare us to our ASEAN neighbors. Di bali na yung

**[01:29:29.450] PTV General Manager Lino S. Cayetano** (asr-002189)  
BBC, di bali na yung NHK, but even Cambodia, even our neighbors spend billions in pesos

**[01:29:39.210] PTV General Manager Lino S. Cayetano** (asr-002190)  
for their state broadcasting system, their communication portals.

**[01:29:44.710] PTV General Manager Lino S. Cayetano** (asr-002191)  
For perspective also po, ang

**[01:29:47.290] PTV General Manager Lino S. Cayetano** (asr-002192)  
private sector po, gagastos yan anywhere from 6 to 10 % of our budget.

**[01:29:52.090] PTV General Manager Lino S. Cayetano** (asr-002193)  
And my heart aches

**[01:29:53.570] PTV General Manager Lino S. Cayetano** (asr-002194)  
when I hear my secretary say that the PCO budget is

**[01:29:56.430] PTV General Manager Lino S. Cayetano** (asr-002195)  
0 .06.

**[01:29:57.790] PTV General Manager Lino S. Cayetano** (asr-002196)  
So ito ho yung nabanggit kanina.

**[01:30:00.000] PTV General Manager Lino S. Cayetano** (asr-002197)  
ng mga kasamaan natin

**[01:30:01.220] PTV General Manager Lino S. Cayetano** (asr-002198)  
na nagpasa ang kongreso

**[01:30:03.040] PTV General Manager Lino S. Cayetano** (asr-002199)  
ng magagandang batas.

**[01:30:04.820] PTV General Manager Lino S. Cayetano** (asr-002200)  
Tapos nun hindi natin mapaliwanag.

**[01:30:06.800] PTV General Manager Lino S. Cayetano** (asr-002201)  
So ito yung developmental communication.

**[01:30:08.840] PTV General Manager Lino S. Cayetano** (asr-002202)  
Developmental communication po

**[01:30:10.200] PTV General Manager Lino S. Cayetano** (asr-002203)  
is applied all over the world.

**[01:30:11.860] PTV General Manager Lino S. Cayetano** (asr-002204)  
It was actually invented

**[01:30:12.860] PTV General Manager Lino S. Cayetano** (asr-002205)  
by a professor in UP Los Baños

**[01:30:15.460] PTV General Manager Lino S. Cayetano** (asr-002206)  
in 1971.

**[01:30:16.980] PTV General Manager Lino S. Cayetano** (asr-002207)  
Ang developmental communication po,

**[01:30:18.860] PTV General Manager Lino S. Cayetano** (asr-002208)  
hindi po to PR,

**[01:30:20.020] PTV General Manager Lino S. Cayetano** (asr-002209)  
hindi po to propaganda.

**[01:30:22.240] PTV General Manager Lino S. Cayetano** (asr-002210)  
It is communicating with the public

**[01:30:24.300] PTV General Manager Lino S. Cayetano** (asr-002211)  
so it can help transform their lives.

**[01:30:26.360] PTV General Manager Lino S. Cayetano** (asr-002212)  
Dahil naiintindihan nila

**[01:30:27.540] PTV General Manager Lino S. Cayetano** (asr-002213)  
yung zero balance billing,

**[01:30:29.360] PTV General Manager Lino S. Cayetano** (asr-002214)  
alam nila na pwede silang

**[01:30:31.040] PTV General Manager Lino S. Cayetano** (asr-002215)  
pumuntang ospital,

**[01:30:32.260] PTV General Manager Lino S. Cayetano** (asr-002216)  
hindi sila mamamatay sa mga bahay nila

**[01:30:34.040] PTV General Manager Lino S. Cayetano** (asr-002217)  
dahil takot sila na mabaon sa utang.

**[01:30:36.260] PTV General Manager Lino S. Cayetano** (asr-002218)  
So, I think it is important

**[01:30:38.540] PTV General Manager Lino S. Cayetano** (asr-002219)  
not just for PTV,

**[01:30:40.160] PTV General Manager Lino S. Cayetano** (asr-002220)  
but to look at the whole ecosystem.

**[01:30:41.900] PTV General Manager Lino S. Cayetano** (asr-002221)  
I know every department has a budget.

**[01:30:44.340] PTV General Manager Lino S. Cayetano** (asr-002222)  
So, hindi naman siguro 0 .06 lang

**[01:30:46.400] PTV General Manager Lino S. Cayetano** (asr-002223)  
ang communications budget

**[02:15:01.680] IBC President Jose C. "Jimmy" Policarpio Jr.** (asr-003438)

**[02:15:18.280] IBC President Jose C. "Jimmy" Policarpio Jr.** (asr-003443)
