**[02:15:21.940] IBC President Jose C. "Jimmy" Policarpio Jr.** (asr-003444)  
So, nung nagsimula po kayo, ilang programa nilunsad nyo, ilan na ngayon?

**[02:15:26.120] IBC President Jose C. "Jimmy" Policarpio Jr.** (asr-003445)  
So, tumalon po?

**[02:15:27.720] IBC President Jose C. "Jimmy" Policarpio Jr.** (asr-003446)  
Tumalon po. Instead, dati po, isa lang, that is tutok.

**[02:15:30.240] IBC President Jose C. "Jimmy" Policarpio Jr.** (asr-003447)  
Yung reach po ng inyong istasyon, I would imagine, dahil dumami yung programa,

**[02:15:35.780] IBC President Jose C. "Jimmy" Policarpio Jr.** (asr-003448)  
lumangan din po yung reach ninyo?

**[02:15:37.700] IBC President Jose C. "Jimmy" Policarpio Jr.** (asr-003449)  
Lumawak naman po, kasi we're now covering 73%.

**[02:15:42.160] IBC President Jose C. "Jimmy" Policarpio Jr.** (asr-003450)  
Oh, mataas po.

**[02:15:42.880] IBC President Jose C. "Jimmy" Policarpio Jr.** (asr-003451)  
Yung tungkol ng maharo sa impeachment, if I may,

**[02:15:45.840] IBC President Jose C. "Jimmy" Policarpio Jr.** (asr-003452)  
ang ginawa ko, in order to put in a layman's term,

**[02:15:49.940] IBC President Jose C. "Jimmy" Policarpio Jr.** (asr-003453)  
I got lawyers.

**[02:15:51.220] IBC President Jose C. "Jimmy" Policarpio Jr.** (asr-003454)  
Para explain kung anong nangyayari sa impeachment,

**[02:15:55.320] IBC President Jose C. "Jimmy" Policarpio Jr.** (asr-003455)  
explains para maintindihan ng mga tao nasa baba.

**[02:15:58.860] IBC President Jose C. "Jimmy" Policarpio Jr.** (asr-003456)  
Ang sa madaling salita pa po, ginagawa po ng IBC 13.

**[02:16:01.900] IBC President Jose C. "Jimmy" Policarpio Jr.** (asr-003457)  
Kayo po yung nagtuturo.

**[02:16:03.760] Rep. Antonino B. Roman III** (asr-003458)  
Tinuturoan nyo po yung tao, pinapalang sa kanila,

**[02:16:05.920] Rep. Antonino B. Roman III** (asr-003459)  
anong ginagawa ng pamalaan.

**[02:16:07.180] Rep. Antonino B. Roman III** (asr-003460)  
Para mas maunawaan at ma-appreciate yung papel

**[02:16:10.060] Rep. Antonino B. Roman III** (asr-003461)  
na ginagampanan ng pamalaan sa kanilang buhay.

**[02:16:14.840] Rep. Antonino B. Roman III** (asr-003462)  
So, ilan po yung empleyado ng IBC 13?

**[02:16:17.860] IBC President Jose C. "Jimmy" Policarpio Jr.** (asr-003463)  
Right now, kukunti lang po, because right now, we have 100,

**[02:16:22.180] IBC President Jose C. "Jimmy" Policarpio Jr.** (asr-003464)  
31, pero meron nyo kaming tinatawag na COS.

**[02:16:25.340] IBC President Jose C. "Jimmy" Policarpio Jr.** (asr-003465)  
Alam nyo naman, 101, sa industry, marami yung COS.

**[02:16:30.580] IBC President Jose C. "Jimmy" Policarpio Jr.** (asr-003466)  
Ilan po sila total, yung employees, yung COS?

**[02:16:32.760] IBC President Jose C. "Jimmy" Policarpio Jr.** (asr-003467)  
Yung total namin, 456, including 100 na regular.

**[02:16:37.620] IBC President Jose C. "Jimmy" Policarpio Jr.** (asr-003468)  
Kasi sa industry, COS yung mga part-time reporter,

**[02:16:43.260] IBC President Jose C. "Jimmy" Policarpio Jr.** (asr-003469)  
kasi nag-introduce kami ng mobile journalism.

**[02:16:48.020] IBC President Jose C. "Jimmy" Policarpio Jr.** (asr-003470)  
Ini-encourage namin yung mga estudyante sa labas,

**[02:16:50.320] IBC President Jose C. "Jimmy" Policarpio Jr.** (asr-003471)  
na gamit ng kanilang cellphone,

**[02:16:53.380] IBC President Jose C. "Jimmy" Policarpio Jr.** (asr-003472)  
pinatuturoan ho namin para gumawa ho sila ng report din,

**[02:16:57.580] IBC President Jose C. "Jimmy" Policarpio Jr.** (asr-003473)  
ipinapadala sa amin, at inaayos naman namin, inilalagay sa lugar.

**[02:17:01.660] IBC President Jose C. "Jimmy" Policarpio Jr.** (asr-003474)  
At ginagawa namin, ninibigyan namin sila ng recognition,

**[02:17:06.180] IBC President Jose C. "Jimmy" Policarpio Jr.** (asr-003475)  
marami na tutuwa.

**[02:17:07.700] IBC President Jose C. "Jimmy" Policarpio Jr.** (asr-003476)  
Karamihan ho dyan, mga state universities.

**[02:17:10.240] IBC President Jose C. "Jimmy" Policarpio Jr.** (asr-003477)  
Sabi din siya, pag sa pondong nakuha nyo,

**[02:17:12.440] IBC President Jose C. "Jimmy" Policarpio Jr.** (asr-003478)  
o da wala kayong pondong nakuha, lahat po yan titigil.

**[02:17:16.420] IBC President Jose C. "Jimmy" Policarpio Jr.** (asr-003479)  
At ano ho ang napektuhan sa mga future,

**[02:17:19.320] IBC President Jose C. "Jimmy" Policarpio Jr.** (asr-003480)  
mga programs ninyo na binala?

**[02:17:22.460] IBC President Jose C. "Jimmy" Policarpio Jr.** (asr-003481)  
Marami ho kami program pa na, meron pong walo.

**[02:17:27.060] IBC President Jose C. "Jimmy" Policarpio Jr.** (asr-003482)  
Kasama po diyan, Senior Moments.

**[02:17:29.920] IBC President Jose C. "Jimmy" Policarpio Jr.** (asr-003483)  
Meron pa po dyan, kinatawag natin mga sexual education.

**[02:17:34.500] IBC President Jose C. "Jimmy" Policarpio Jr.** (asr-003484)  
Kasi napapansin ko, marami mga bata na, bata pa lang, nabubuntis.

**[02:17:39.100] IBC President Jose C. "Jimmy" Policarpio Jr.** (asr-003485)  
So we would like to humanize it, explain.

**[02:17:41.360] IBC President Jose C. "Jimmy" Policarpio Jr.** (asr-003486)  
Opo. Actually, yung nakita ko po.

**[02:17:43.580] IBC President Jose C. "Jimmy" Policarpio Jr.** (asr-003487)  
Meron po kami, opo, and mano po.

**[02:17:46.640] IBC President Jose C. "Jimmy" Policarpio Jr.** (asr-003488)  
Being in back Filipino culture.

**[02:17:49.540] IBC President Jose C. "Jimmy" Policarpio Jr.** (asr-003490)  
Nung tumira po ako sa Amerika nang mahabang panahon.

**[02:17:54.180] IBC President Jose C. "Jimmy" Policarpio Jr.** (asr-003491)  
Ako ay sumusubaybay sa C-SPAN.

**[02:17:57.160] IBC President Jose C. "Jimmy" Policarpio Jr.** (asr-003492)  
Nung nakita ko po yung Congress TV,

**[02:17:59.100] IBC President Jose C. "Jimmy" Policarpio Jr.** (asr-003493)  
iyon po, nakita ko yung papel na ginagampanan ninyo,

**[02:18:01.420] IBC President Jose C. "Jimmy" Policarpio Jr.** (asr-003494)  
na dinadala nyo dahil ang kaalaman po ay nasa kapalad po ng mga tao.

**[02:18:08.580] IBC President Jose C. "Jimmy" Policarpio Jr.** (asr-003495)  
Korotorin to, eto po, pinapalad ko po yung proceedings ngayon.

**[02:18:12.160] IBC President Jose C. "Jimmy" Policarpio Jr.** (asr-003496)  
Nakikita ko, nalalaman nila kung anong ginagawa ng mga nasa Kongreso.

**[02:18:17.320] IBC President Jose C. "Jimmy" Policarpio Jr.** (asr-003497)  
Mahalaga, mahalaga po.

**[02:18:18.460] IBC President Jose C. "Jimmy" Policarpio Jr.** (asr-003498)  
We made sure, Your Honor.

**[02:18:19.860] IBC President Jose C. "Jimmy" Policarpio Jr.** (asr-003499)  
As a matter of fact, may dagdag ko lang po.

**[02:18:22.180] IBC President Jose C. "Jimmy" Policarpio Jr.** (asr-003500)  
We have 90 under Congress.

**[02:18:23.980] IBC President Jose C. "Jimmy" Policarpio Jr.** (asr-003501)  
Kasi marami po tayong mga nagko-cover sa probinsya, dito po.

**[02:18:28.880] IBC President Jose C. "Jimmy" Policarpio Jr.** (asr-003502)  
Lahat po ng hearing covered natin.

**[02:18:31.920] IBC President Jose C. "Jimmy" Policarpio Jr.** (asr-003503)  
Ano ang balak ninyo para mabayaran ninyo iyong legal obligation?

**[02:18:36.700] IBC President Jose C. "Jimmy" Policarpio Jr.** (asr-003504)  
Dala ng

**[02:18:37.320] IBC President Jose C. "Jimmy" Policarpio Jr.** (asr-003505)  
58 million po ba?

**[02:18:41.360] IBC President Jose C. "Jimmy" Policarpio Jr.** (asr-003506)  
50 million po. Kasama po yan sa nirequest namin.

**[02:18:44.120] IBC President Jose C. "Jimmy" Policarpio Jr.** (asr-003507)  
Sa madaling salita, hindi na ho mababayaran iyon.

**[02:18:47.520] IBC President Jose C. "Jimmy" Policarpio Jr.** (asr-003508)  
Hindi mo mababayaran pag di po kami binigyan.

**[02:18:49.880] IBC President Jose C. "Jimmy" Policarpio Jr.** (asr-003509)  
Hindi.

**[02:18:50.060] IBC President Jose C. "Jimmy" Policarpio Jr.** (asr-003510)  
Hindi po sasalunan yung ibang ahensya diyan sa ilalim ng PCO?

**[02:18:53.780] IBC President Jose C. "Jimmy" Policarpio Jr.** (asr-003511)  
Ganito po nangyari nung matagal na hindi nababayaran yung mga more than 350 retirees since 1998.

**[02:19:04.760] IBC President Jose C. "Jimmy" Policarpio Jr.** (asr-003512)  
Nagbalak pa po silang magpunta sa Human Rights Commission para i-report.

**[02:19:09.820] IBC President Jose C. "Jimmy" Policarpio Jr.** (asr-003513)  
Sabi ko, no, no, no, no. That's out of the question.

**[02:19:13.100] IBC President Jose C. "Jimmy" Policarpio Jr.** (asr-003514)  
Ganoon, aayusin natin iyan.

**[02:19:14.120] IBC President Jose C. "Jimmy" Policarpio Jr.** (asr-003515)  
Kamusta naman po yung sentimiento ng kulang limandaang empleyado at COS ninyo

**[02:19:19.180] IBC President Jose C. "Jimmy" Policarpio Jr.** (asr-003516)  
nang nalaman nilang walang pondo po ang IBC-13?

**[02:19:21.340] IBC President Jose C. "Jimmy" Policarpio Jr.** (asr-003517)  
Masayang-masaya po sila, yung lahat po ng retirees.

**[02:19:24.520] IBC President Jose C. "Jimmy" Policarpio Jr.** (asr-003518)  
Kasi lalo na po yung mga namatayan na, at least po nabayaran yung retirement na kunilang ama,

**[02:19:30.920] IBC President Jose C. "Jimmy" Policarpio Jr.** (asr-003519)  
nabayaran po yung mga utang, at higit sa lahat po tinulungan namin ma-repair niyang papanong ma-write off na lang yung iba.

**[02:19:38.580] IBC President Jose C. "Jimmy" Policarpio Jr.** (asr-003520)  
Di po, ang tanong ko po, kamusta po ang kulang limandaang empleyado at COS ng IBC-13

**[02:19:45.820] IBC President Jose C. "Jimmy" Policarpio Jr.** (asr-003521)  
nang nalaman nila na wala po kayong pondo itong taon na ito?

**[02:19:50.440] IBC President Jose C. "Jimmy" Policarpio Jr.** (asr-003522)  
Naniniwala po sila sa puso at damdamin ng ating pamahalaan, ng ating lehislatibo.

**[02:19:59.440] Rep. Antonino B. Roman III** (asr-003523)  
Maasa po sila.

**[02:20:01.000] Rep. Antonino B. Roman III** (asr-003524)  
Opo, sana po, katulad naman sinabi ng mga kasamahan ko, sana po, mabigyan ng pondo ang IB13.

**[02:20:08.880] Rep. Antonino B. Roman III** (asr-003525)  
Nakita naman natin kung saan sila nagsimula at nasan sila ngayon.

**[02:20:12.840] Rep. Antonino B. Roman III** (asr-003526)  
Sabi nga ni Cong. Brian Yamsuan, hanggang sa huli, palagi.

**[02:20:18.480] Rep. Antonino B. Roman III** (asr-003527)  
Di ba?

**[02:20:19.620] Rep. Antonino B. Roman III** (asr-003528)  
At sabi naman ni Chair, ni Madam Chair, ipaglalaban.

**[02:20:23.900] Rep. Antonino B. Roman III** (asr-003529)  
Mga programa sa TVA, ipaglaban mo.

**[02:20:26.860] Rep. Antonino B. Roman III** (asr-003530)  
Di ba? So, paglalaban natin yan.

**[02:20:32.170] Rep. Antonino B. Roman III** (asr-003531)  
At sa PIA, naku, nung nakita ko yung pictures ng mga bats, alam niyo, ibang-iba eh.

**[02:20:40.730] Rep. Antonino B. Roman III** (asr-003532)  
Because I live in two different cultures, I see the difference. It's night and day.

**[02:20:45.030] Rep. Antonino B. Roman III** (asr-003533)  
Bakit kung sa Amerika, kapag ahensya ng pamahalaan ng departamento,

**[02:20:51.010] Rep. Antonino B. Roman III** (asr-003534)  
napaka-impressive ang mga gusali, malinis,

**[02:20:54.210] Rep. Antonino B. Roman III** (asr-003535)  
matibay, malamig, maganda yung mga furniture.

**[02:20:57.130] Rep. Antonino B. Roman III** (asr-003536)  
Bakit sa atin, kapag gobyerno, parang hindi inaalagaan ang bahay?

**[02:21:04.350] Rep. Antonino B. Roman III** (asr-003537)  
Hindi ko maunawaan.

**[02:21:05.790] Rep. Antonino B. Roman III** (asr-003538)  
And I believe that if you want to change the culture, you want to promote professionalism,

**[02:21:13.590] Rep. Antonino B. Roman III** (asr-003539)  
you need a professional environment.

**[02:21:16.270] Rep. Antonino B. Roman III** (asr-003540)  
And certainly what I saw is not a professional environment.

**[02:21:19.030] Rep. Antonino B. Roman III** (asr-003541)  
Sana ho, mapagtuunan ng pansin yun.

**[02:21:21.850] Rep. Antonino B. Roman III** (asr-003542)  
Kaya nung bagong congressman ako,

**[02:21:23.890] Rep. Antonino B. Roman III** (asr-003543)  
nung ginawa, pina-renovate ko ang aking opisina,

**[02:21:27.030] Rep. Antonino B. Roman III** (asr-003544)  
na sa mahabang panahon, ilang dekada, hindi na-renovate.

**[02:21:31.730] Rep. Antonino B. Roman III** (asr-003545)  
Kasi kailangan ko ng magandang environment para makapagtrabaho ng mabuti at paglingkuran ang mamamayan.

**[02:21:38.110] Rep. Antonino B. Roman III** (asr-003546)  
At sana ganun po gawin natin sa PIA.

**[02:21:40.590] Rep. Antonino B. Roman III** (asr-003547)  
Maraming salamat po.

**[02:21:41.530] Rep. Alexandria "Queenie" Gonzales** (asr-003548)  
Thank you very much, Representative Antonino Roman.

**[02:21:45.230] Rep. Alexandria "Queenie" Gonzales** (asr-003549)  
Our chair calls for a momentary suspension.

**[02:35:14.030] Rep. Alexandria "Queenie" Gonzales** (asr-003550)  
The committee resumes the hearing for House Bill No. 10858.

**[02:35:21.130] Rep. Antonino B. Roman III** (asr-003551)  
Madam Chair.

**[02:35:23.610] Rep. Alexandria "Queenie" Gonzales** (asr-003551-split-1)  
The committee now recognizes Representative Antonino Roman for additional interpellation.

**[02:35:29.590] Rep. Antonino B. Roman III** (asr-003552)  
For additional interpellation, I’m just curious on, would like to know the reason for defunding IBC 13.

**[02:35:35.670] Rep. Antonino B. Roman III** (asr-003553)  
Bakit ito nawala ng pondo?

**[02:35:39.680] Rep. Antonino B. Roman III** (asr-003554)  
Secretary Dave.

**[02:35:41.180] Secretary David M. Gomez** (asr-003554-split-1)  
Yes, thank you, Your Honor.

**[02:35:43.120] Secretary David M. Gomez** (asr-003555)  
And if again, I may reiterate what I mentioned earlier, we were really very surprised and very saddened when we saw the NEP that was

**[02:35:55.840] Secretary David M. Gomez** (asr-003556)  
submitted to Congress that allocated zero budget for IBC, only to find out that the

**[02:36:03.240] Secretary David M. Gomez** (asr-003557)  
rationale, the reason behind it was the supposed planned privatization of the station.

**[02:36:08.540] Secretary David M. Gomez** (asr-003558)  
We are working, as I mentioned earlier as well, we are working for the privatization of the station.

**[02:36:16.300] Secretary David M. Gomez** (asr-003559)  
And in fact, we're meeting with the GCG next week to finalize the plans.

**[02:36:20.660] Secretary David M. Gomez** (asr-003560)  
But there are several working pieces that we need to put in place first.

**[02:36:28.680] Secretary David M. Gomez** (asr-003561)  
But we're really looking to complete the process by end of the year, but...

**[02:36:35.500] Secretary David M. Gomez** (asr-003562)  
But if

**[02:36:37.160] Secretary David M. Gomez** (asr-003563)  
there are some headwinds that we may encounter, obstacles thrown our way,

**[02:36:42.620] Secretary David M. Gomez** (asr-003564)  
what happens to the station if they don't have any budget by first of January next year? What happens to our employees?

**[02:36:51.020] Secretary David M. Gomez** (asr-003565)  
What happens to our programs? That's why we're really appealing to this honorable committee

**[02:37:00.400] Secretary David M. Gomez** (asr-003566)  
to restore what IBC 13 proposed in their National Expenditure Program.

**[02:37:06.960] Rep. Antonino B. Roman III** (asr-003567)  
So, sa madaling salita po, nasa initial stages pa lang ng planning,

**[02:37:10.940] Rep. Antonino B. Roman III** (asr-003568)  
na malayo pa ho ang mahabang panahon pa ang lilipas bago ma-finalize?

**[02:37:17.860] Secretary David M. Gomez** (asr-003569)  
Hindi naman po, Your Honor, hindi naman po.

**[02:37:19.780] Secretary David M. Gomez** (asr-003570)  
Medyo, simula nung dumating ako dito sa role na ito, nung tinignan namin, medyo matagal na stall, matagal na stall yung privatization initiative. Only now that we are pushing with guidance from the top that we really should prioritize this.

**[02:37:39.180] Secretary David M. Gomez** (asr-003571)  
That we are working now with the station, DBP, and GCG so that finally before the end of the year,

**[02:37:48.680] Secretary David M. Gomez** (asr-003572)  
we can call for an open public bidding for interested parties.

**[02:37:53.700] Rep. Antonino B. Roman III** (asr-003573)  
So malayo po tayo sa bidding, maaaring mag-fail pa yung bidding, marami pang puwedeng mangyari.

**[02:38:00.300] Rep. Antonino B. Roman III** (asr-003574)  
Maaaring mawalan ng interes sa mga kinakausap ninyo. Marami pa kayong kakausapin.

**[02:38:05.340] Rep. Antonino B. Roman III** (asr-003575)  
So mukhang mahabang panahon po, ang maraming... ilang buwan pang lilipas bago ma-finalize ito.

**[02:38:12.300] Rep. Antonino B. Roman III** (asr-003576)  
So masasabi n’yo po na parang premature at napaaga masyado yung pagtanggal ng pondo sa inyo.

**[02:38:18.540] Secretary David M. Gomez** (asr-003577)  
Opo your honor totoo po.

**[02:38:21.620] Rep. Alexandria "Queenie" Gonzales** (asr-003577-split-1)  
Maraming salamat, Congressman Roman. Um,

**[02:38:25.640] Rep. Alexandria "Queenie" Gonzales** (asr-003578)  
habang tayo po ay nagsasabi na lahat po tayo ay talagang nadurog ang puso

**[02:38:32.280] Rep. Alexandria "Queenie" Gonzales** (asr-003579)  
nang nakita natin ang IBC 13 na nag-zero budget,

**[02:38:35.740] Rep. Alexandria "Queenie" Gonzales** (asr-003580)  
nandito lang po pala sa in our midst si DBM Assistant Director Lulu P. Vispo.

**[02:38:43.740] Rep. Alexandria "Queenie" Gonzales** (asr-003581)  
Siya po ay puwede natin tanungin as to the wisdom of the DBM, why it came to be that IBC had zero budget for 2027.

**[02:38:53.200] Rep. Alexandria "Queenie" Gonzales** (asr-003582)  
Ma’am, we recognize Assistant Director Lulu.

**[02:38:56.820] DBM Assistant Director Lulu P. Vispo** (asr-003583)  
Good morning, Madam Chair and Your Honor.

**[02:39:01.280] DBM Assistant Director Lulu P. Vispo** (asr-003584)  
Actually, IBC 13 is really currently under the GOCC, which is listed as under privatization.

**[02:39:10.960] DBM Assistant Director Lulu P. Vispo** (asr-003585)  
This is pursuant to the memo of the Executive Secretary, dated December 21, 2015.

**[02:39:20.360] DBM Assistant Director Lulu P. Vispo** (asr-003586)  
So, up to now, yun ang tingin ng DBM because it is an ongoing privatization.

**[02:39:27.200] DBM Assistant Director Lulu P. Vispo** (asr-003587)  
But for 2026...

**[02:39:28.200] DBM Assistant Director Lulu P. Vispo** (asr-003588)  
We have released 424 million for the compensation benefits of the IBC 13 employees.

**[02:39:40.820] DBM Assistant Director Lulu P. Vispo** (asr-003589)  
So, kung baga, sinusuportahan din naman ng DBM if ever na need talaga na like that.

**[02:39:48.360] DBM Assistant Director Lulu P. Vispo** (asr-003590)  
And ang assumption namin is, last na na po yun, nababayaran namin.

**[02:39:53.460] DBM Assistant Director Lulu P. Vispo** (asr-003591)  
considering na yung mga retirement benefits na nabayaran na rin namin.

**[02:39:56.920] DBM Assistant Director Lulu P. Vispo** (asr-003592)  
And then, when you speak of yung...

**[02:39:59.700] DBM Assistant Director Lulu P. Vispo** (asr-003593)  
arbitration award, which is in the amount of 50 million, I think that was awarded only in June this year.

**[02:40:07.580] DBM Assistant Director Lulu P. Vispo** (asr-003594)  
E tapos na po ang budget preparation that time.

**[02:40:10.380] DBM Assistant Director Lulu P. Vispo** (asr-003595)  
And nag-re-issue na po kami noon ng confirmation letter.

**[02:40:14.000] DBM Assistant Director Lulu P. Vispo** (asr-003596)  
So, possible po talagang hindi siya maisasama sa 2027.

**[02:40:20.000] DBM Assistant Director Lulu P. Vispo** (asr-003597)  
That's our position po.

**[02:40:23.200] DBM Assistant Director Lulu P. Vispo** (asr-003598)  
Madam Chair.

**[02:40:25.560] Rep. Antonino B. Roman III** (asr-003599)  
Thank you, Assistant Director.

**[02:40:27.460] Rep. Antonino B. Roman III** (asr-003600)  
Madam Chair, is she a resource person also from DBM?

**[02:40:31.040] Rep. Alexandria "Queenie" Gonzales** (asr-003601)  
Yes, she is assistant, yes.

**[02:40:33.580] Rep. Antonino B. Roman III** (asr-003602)  
She's a resource person, therefore I can propound questions to her.

**[02:40:36.980] Rep. Alexandria "Queenie" Gonzales** (asr-003603)  
Yes, yes, go ahead, Representative Antonino.

**[02:40:40.260] Rep. Antonino B. Roman III** (asr-003604)  
So, alam niyo yung arbitration award na kailangan mo yan ng 50 million.

**[02:40:44.520] Rep. Antonino B. Roman III** (asr-003605)  
Tapos, sa kabila nun, dinefund niyo

**[02:40:46.780] Rep. Antonino B. Roman III** (asr-003606)  
So, sino magbabayad noon?

**[02:40:50.620] DBM Assistant Director Lulu P. Vispo** (asr-003607)  
Actually, sir, we don't receive any...

**[02:40:57.740] DBM Assistant Director Lulu P. Vispo** (asr-003608)  
I mean, we don't need any record.

**[02:41:01.240] DBM Assistant Director Lulu P. Vispo** (asr-003609)  
Yeah, we did not receive any record

**[02:41:03.120] DBM Assistant Director Lulu P. Vispo** (asr-003610)  
or any request on that.

**[02:41:04.940] DBM Assistant Director Lulu P. Vispo** (asr-003611)  
And we don't have any copy of that arbitration award.

**[02:41:08.800] Rep. Antonino B. Roman III** (asr-003612)  
But the DBM was present in the last year's budget deliberations.

**[02:41:13.020] Rep. Antonino B. Roman III** (asr-003613)  
And that was one of the issues lengthily discussed during the budget deliberations of PCO.

**[02:41:21.840] DBM Assistant Director Lulu P. Vispo** (asr-003614)  
That's why, sir, in 2026, the IBC 13 requested for the compensation benefits.

**[02:41:30.020] DBM Assistant Director Lulu P. Vispo** (asr-003615)  
And we granted it out of the Pension Gratuity Fund.

**[02:41:33.400] DBM Assistant Director Lulu P. Vispo** (asr-003616)  
In the amount of 428 million.

**[02:41:36.440] Rep. Antonino B. Roman III** (asr-003617)  
But you just said that you found out about the arbitration award came down in June of this year.

**[02:41:44.100] Rep. Antonino B. Roman III** (asr-003618)  
This year.

**[02:41:45.180] DBM Assistant Director Lulu P. Vispo** (asr-003619)  
Yes.

**[02:41:45.580] Rep. Antonino B. Roman III** (asr-003620)  
And you said it's too late.

**[02:41:49.520] DBM Assistant Director Lulu P. Vispo** (asr-003621)  
And the budget prep season is from April up to May.

**[02:41:54.200] DBM Assistant Director Lulu P. Vispo** (asr-003622)  
And June is already a final deliberation.

**[02:41:58.060] DBM Assistant Director Lulu P. Vispo** (asr-003623)  
And we have to issue our confirmation letter to the agencies.

**[02:42:02.690] DBM Assistant Director Lulu P. Vispo** (asr-003624)  
Tapos na po.

**[02:42:03.530] Rep. Antonino B. Roman III** (asr-003625)  
But given that this supervening event...

**[02:42:05.910] Rep. Antonino B. Roman III** (asr-003626)  
Don't you think that requires an amendment?

**[02:42:09.460] Rep. Antonino B. Roman III** (asr-003627)  
To satisfy legal obligations?

**[02:42:12.920] DBM Assistant Director Lulu P. Vispo** (asr-003628)  
Sir, it's up to the Congress, po.

**[02:42:16.360] Rep. Antonino B. Roman III** (asr-003629)  
Pero don't you think it's wrong for government not to pay legal obligations?

**[02:42:19.980] Rep. Antonino B. Roman III** (asr-003630)  
That government should set an example?

**[02:42:22.120] DBM Assistant Director Lulu P. Vispo** (asr-003631)  
Sir, like what we did in 2026 when they requested for the compensation benefits of those IBC employees,

**[02:42:30.440] DBM Assistant Director Lulu P. Vispo** (asr-003632)  
we granted them 428 million.

**[02:42:33.020] DBM Assistant Director Lulu P. Vispo** (asr-003633)  
And that is charged against the Pension and Gratuity Fund, po.

**[02:42:36.120] Rep. Antonino B. Roman III** (asr-003634)  
Were you present earlier?

**[02:42:37.220] Rep. Antonino B. Roman III** (asr-003635)  
During the discussions here?

**[02:42:40.940] DBM Assistant Director Lulu P. Vispo** (asr-003636)  
Yes.

**[02:42:41.620] Rep. Antonino B. Roman III** (asr-003637)  
Yes, you were.

**[02:42:42.400] Rep. Antonino B. Roman III** (asr-003638)  
You heard the sentiments and the views of the congressmen here?

**[02:42:45.180] DBM Assistant Director Lulu P. Vispo** (asr-003639)  
Yes, Your Honor.

**[02:42:46.280] Rep. Antonino B. Roman III** (asr-003640)  
And you appreciate where we're coming from?

**[02:42:49.320] Rep. Antonino B. Roman III** (asr-003641)  
Okay.

**[02:42:50.280] Rep. Antonino B. Roman III** (asr-003642)  
Maraming salamat po.

**[02:42:53.640] Rep. Alexandria "Queenie" Gonzales** (asr-003643)

**[02:43:00.840] Rep. Alexandria "Queenie" Gonzales** (asr-003644)  
remedy?

**[02:43:01.960] Rep. Alexandria "Queenie" Gonzales** (asr-003645)

**[02:43:06.100] Rep. Alexandria "Queenie" Gonzales** (asr-003646)  
So, just...

**[02:43:06.721] DBM Assistant Director Lulu P. Vispo** (asr-003647)

**[02:43:11.520] DBM Assistant Director Lulu P. Vispo** (asr-003648)  
Wala ho kaming copy. Kaya we did not, we didn't know na meron palang gano'n na. Yes, yes po.

**[02:43:18.400] Secretary David M. Gomez** (asr-003649)  
Madam Chairman, if I may add, no, I will concur with the report of the Director.

**[02:43:26.720] Secretary David M. Gomez** (asr-003650)  
By June 30 this year, tapos na po yung preparation ng NEP, no, na-discuss na po sa cabinet, na-present na po sa Pangulo yun.

**[02:43:34.000] Secretary David M. Gomez** (asr-003651)  
So, medyo, hindi nga siguro umabot, no.

**[02:43:37.580] Secretary David M. Gomez** (asr-003652)  
But tama din po si Congressman na

**[02:43:40.180] Secretary David M. Gomez** (asr-003653)  
This is a legal obligation that IBC 13 needs to settle.

**[02:43:43.900] Secretary David M. Gomez** (asr-003654)  
So, isasama na lang po namin doon sa summary namin of the budget

**[02:43:49.280] Secretary David M. Gomez** (asr-003655)  
proposed by the attached agencies that were cut or were not included, but we respectfully

**[02:43:55.200] Secretary David M. Gomez** (asr-003656)

**[02:43:58.160] Secretary David M. Gomez** (asr-003657)  
Salamat, po.

**[02:43:59.280] Rep. Alexandria "Queenie" Gonzales** (asr-003658)  
Thank you very much, Secretary Dave.

**[02:44:01.360] Rep. Alexandria "Queenie" Gonzales** (asr-003659)

**[02:44:06.620] Rep. Alexandria "Queenie" Gonzales** (asr-003660)  
committee.

**[02:44:08.540] Rep. Alexandria "Queenie" Gonzales** (asr-003661)  
Okay.

**[02:44:09.200] Rep. Alexandria "Queenie" Gonzales** (asr-003662)  
So, um...
